    - **Try it** closes the dialog and opens the REAL Snooze menu, the same way the person's own click does, and never picks a time. With a thread open, it opens that thread's header ⋯ menu (on a phone, its ⋯ sheet), swapped to its times. With no thread open, it opens the right-click menu of the row just left, swapped to its times (on a phone, it opens that thread again, then its ⋯ sheet). No new keyboard shortcut.
    - Tests: `packages/inbox/snoozeIntroRows.test.ts` (read and left, the need, waits on the person), `packages/server/inbox-snooze-intro.test.ts` (the 2-day boundary, each condition, first use; the upgrade backfill, which runs once and also covers a snooze running at the upgrade; a fresh install's later fill; dev builds; two claims at once; 405 and 409; a guest never due nor claiming, the owner's phone claiming; the snoozed mark from the window, a tailnet owner but never a guest, the phone app through the door, a linked machine's forward, and a dev build, only once the snooze succeeded), `packages/inbox/snoozeIntroDialog.test.tsx` (DOM: the calm moment and each blocker, a draft reaching the list after the leave (before the claim, and while it is on its way), App's "another one-time dialog this load" wiring, the list settle, each dismiss stamped and final, a reload, and Try it against the real `ThreadSnooze`, phone `ThreadMore` and `ThreadRowMenu` without picking a time), `tests/e2e/inbox-snooze-intro.spec.ts` (a compiled release binary seeded by `inbox-snooze-intro-seed.ts`: never on load, once at the moment, a second tab and a reload never, Try it on desktop and on a 390 px phone snoozes nothing; shots in `PLANNOTATOR_E2E_PROOF_DIR/snooze-intro`).
  - **The one-time "themes" dialog** (approval "inbox-theme-chooser", 2026-11-10, `.product/approved/inbox-theme-chooser/`: the mock, its stills and build notes; `components/ThemeChooser.tsx`, the page's hook and the dialog's model in `themeChooser.ts`, who gets it in `packages/shared/inbox/theme-chooser.ts`). It offers the two Plannotator themes, and the way to the rest, to someone who never picked a theme.
    - Who: only someone on the default theme, Plannotator Original on both halves (config.json `theme` with no `light` and no `dark`, or both `plannotator`; the mode is not part of it). The server decides at the first release start of the version that ships it and keeps the answer in config.json `inboxThemeChooser`: `dueAt` on the default theme (a fresh install too, from day one), `notNeededAt` on any other theme. `notNeededAt` is also written at any later look that finds another theme (each start, a theme saved from the Inbox, a claim), and it is final: going back to the default theme never brings the dialog up. A dev build decides nothing. Owner pages only.
    - When (the owner, 2026-11-10: "Fifteen seconds after the inbox opens, but it can't compete with the favicon one… The favicon one should come first. Themes must come only after, like thirty minutes after. If favicon's already done, themes can show up fifteen seconds after opening"): it is a dialog of the line below, `theme_chooser`, the entry right AFTER `tab_icon` in `INBOX_REVEALS`, `kind: "update"` (it never retires and has no Not now), no tenure and no thread minimum (day one is fine). The server lists it in `lineOffers` only while it is still for this person (`themeChooserOpen`, read live, so a palette picked anywhere drops it) AND the tab icon dialog is not still due, so patience can never let it pass that one; after the tab icon dialog shows it waits the line's own gap (`REVEAL_SPACING_MS`, 40 minutes, unchanged: the owner's "like thirty minutes after" has no number of its own), as after any other dialog or tip. No other dialog's who or when moved. Code review's offer keeps its place and its own moment. The page (`useThemeChooser`, on `useLineReveal` with `atOnce`) opens it 15 s into the page (`REVEAL_TIMES.soonPageAgeMs`, `soonMayClaim`), the tab visible and the list loaded, with NO thread to read and leave and NO stillness: a pointer that never rests does not postpone it (the first build waited for the line's 60 s and then for a page left still, and the owner never saw it). The tab icon dialog has the same moment now. What still holds it back is exactly what Code review's at-once offer has: `clear` (the Inbox page, the list with or without a thread open, no file, document, guided review or Artifacts view over it, no search, no New message, no update dialog or restart), `revealBlocked` (the tour or an update on this load, another one-time dialog or tip up or claimed), `revealHeldBack` (a dialog or `aria-modal` panel open, an image full size, a pinned reference card, a menu, a toast, a text field focused, a reply box holding unsent words or files, a question or action item waiting in the open thread) and the 2.5 s settling wait after the list or the place changes. A hold only makes it wait: a moment that lapses is made again, so it opens once the person stops typing and the box is empty or sent. With the tab left loaded, the page reads the line again when the gap ends (`reveal.again_in`, `useLineLatch`) and opens it then. A palette picked on this page drops it (`holds`). It never stacks: its stage is in `otherRevealActive`, `stagesBusy` and `linesFree`.
    - The dialog: the title is "themes" as six letter tiles, each tile a real theme's background and primary (`THEME_CHOOSER_TILES`; the face is the star ask's Archivo subset, `starAskFonts.ts`; the heading's name is "Themes"). Under it, a fan of three cards: Plannotator Original (in front on open), Plannotator Modern, and "Choose your own". A theme card holds two real screenshots, its light and its dark: the one for the system's mode in front, the other out at the top right corner under a small "light" / "dark" button. Pointing at that corner (70 ms of rest; it re-arms only after the pointer leaves it), tapping it, or Enter or Space on its button brings that sheet forward. Pointing at another card, tapping it, focusing one of its buttons, Left and Right on a card's button, or a swipe on a touch screen brings that card forward. "Choose your own" shows three more themes and "N more themes" (the registry's count less two).
    - What a pick saves (`themeChooserPick`): the theme on BOTH halves, and the mode. The sheet in front is the system's mode (never shuffled, or shuffled back): Match system, and the line says "follows your system". The other sheet is in front: that mode, fixed ("always dark" / "always light"). The button says "Keep Original" only when nothing would change, else "Use Modern, light". The write is Settings' own (`useInboxThemePick` in `theme.ts`: the Colors row's `choose` for each half and the Theme row's for the mode), so it applies at once and only the parts that changed go to `POST /api/inbox/settings` `theme`. "Open Settings" closes the dialog and opens `#settings/general` with the Colors row in view and its tab focused (`openThemeSettings`). The close button, Escape and a click outside change nothing. Every way out is final: the claim stamped it.
    - Motion (the build notes; `THEME_CHOOSER_MOTION`): a card comes forward in 600 ms (the owner: "six hundred milliseconds. That's final"), the others go back in 516 ms, each 60 ms later per step of distance; the shuffle takes 540 ms. Only transform and opacity animate. All three cards are in one 3D context and a card's size comes from its depth, so no z-index changes between cards; the card a pointer means is worked out from where the cards are going (`themeChooserCardAt`), so a move can be redirected mid-way. The shuffle is two Web Animations that can be reversed in place. The custom properties are `--thc-*`: `inbox.css` registers short names (`--d`, `--e`), and reusing one computes the transition to `all 0s`. With reduced motion every duration is 0 and the behaviour is the same. At 520 px or less the cards are a deck in a bottom sheet (`themeChooserGeometry`).
    - Pictures: seven desktop and four phone WebP files in `packages/inbox/assets/theme-chooser/` (about 460 KB), each set in its own script (`themeChooserArtFan.ts`, `themeChooserArtDeck.ts`, `themeChooserArtOwn.ts`). `loadThemeChooserArt` imports them only when the dialog mounts (the claim is spent), and decodes every picture before the dialog draws; a page that never shows the dialog fetches none of it. Retake them with `bun scripts/theme-chooser-shots.ts` after `bun run build:hook`: it starts a scratch Inbox of its own, writes made-up threads and shoots each theme in a headless Chromium.
    - No keyboard shortcut. Focus starts on the front card's button, stays in the dialog (`Modal`), and goes back where it was.
    - Tests: `packages/shared/inbox/theme-chooser.test.ts` (who; right after the tab icon dialog; the line's gap after it; day one), `packages/server/inbox-reveal.test.ts` ("the themes dialog": due and claimed once, marked on another theme, a palette picked here or elsewhere, a fresh install, a guest), `packages/inbox/themeChooser.test.tsx` (DOM: 15 s into the page with the pointer never at rest, the claim, no picture before it, what each pick writes, the corner by keyboard, a shuffle turned round, the arrow keys, each way out, reduced motion, the phone deck).
  - **The schedule of one-time dialogs** (approval "inbox-one-time-dialogs", 2026-11-06, `.product/approved/inbox-one-time-dialogs/`; Inbox only, plan and code review untouched). Every one-time dialog of the Inbox but the tour and the notification ask is in one line, the reveals' queue (`packages/shared/inbox/reveals.ts`, below), which the server keeps and every claim checks:
    - The queue, in order (the owner's, 2026-11-10: "Alright, let's ship it"): Code review's day-0 offer (below; not shipped). Then the SAME-DAY group, none with a tenure, so each may show on a person's first day, the gap (40 minutes) apart, as its own "who" holds: 1 the tab icon dialog (15 s into the page), 2 the "themes" dialog (15 s into the page, never while the tab icon dialog is due), 3 List view, "Grouping" (`list_view`: no 2-day wait any more; `minThreads: 15` is still the server's stand-in for "taller than the screen", which only the page sees and checks at its moment), 4 list options, "Rows" (`list_intro`, 5 threads), 5 the sound (only after an update that adds it), 6 voice (only with a `codex` CLI). Then the LATER group, later only by `tenureMs`: 7 Snooze (2 days), 8 the Board (3 days, 5 threads), 9 the Checklist (5 days, 5 threads), 10 Bro mode (2 days, heavy use; after the Checklist in order). Nothing but tenure separates the two groups: there is no one-a-day rule. An `ahead` entry (List view) never holds the line (`revealNext` picks the first other qualifying entry and `reveal.ahead` arms List view beside it; it is `next` only when nothing else is), so a list that is not taller than the screen, or a person on Simple list, never keeps Rows, the sound or Snooze waiting; it is armed beside an offer anywhere and beside an update-day dialog only when that one stands after it (so never beside the tab icon or themes dialogs). The star ask and the grill-me tip stand aside (below). `GET /api/inbox/settings` `reveal.next` names the one a load may arm (`sound`, `list_intro` and `voice` too; the page shows each only when its own `due` holds and it is `next`, App's `lineArms`; `reveal.line` is true on a release build, and a dev build or an older Inbox, without it, keeps each dialog's own `due`). `reveal.ahead` names the offers marked `ahead` (List view, once registered) that a load may also arm: each goes as soon as its own moment comes, ahead of the offers before it (the approval's change: "once the list is taller than the screen, at the next calm moment"), still inside the load and spacing rules, and never before an update-day dialog (`revealAhead`). The agent note is a setup dialog outside the queue: `reveal.setup` says whether this load may show it, and the page claims it (`POST /api/inbox/reveal` `agent_note`, with the agents it would ask about) before it opens. It shows once for each agent (`inboxReveals.agentNote.shownFor`, `revealAgentNoteNew`): a note left unanswered (the tab closed) is not shown again for the same agents, so it never takes another day's place; a newly connected agent is asked about.
    - Rules (release builds; a dev build keeps no line): day 0 is setup only (offers need tenure; the tour, then the agent note once the tour's screens are gone (since "inbox-agent-note-auto" only for Qwen Code or Cursor: the proven agents' notes are written by themselves and said by the connect line above the list, which is no dialog and takes no slot), `!showTourEntry && !tourIntroShown`, then the notification lines, which already wait for the note); no one-a-day rule since 2026-11-09 (the owner: "I don't wanna wait a whole day now ... at 40 minutes, give him the new option"; `dayShownAt` is now only the agent note's stamp for the gap); no page-load rule since 2026-11-09 either (the owner: "after 40 minutes, the same tab (or a fresh load) can show the next one"; it was "never on two page loads in a row", and a load that showed a dialog showed no other; each page still sends its load id, `X-Plannotator-Load`, and a claim keeps it as `inboxReveals.shownLoad`, a record nothing reads); 40 minutes between any two dialogs (`REVEAL_SPACING_MS`, `revealGapFree`: one clock read from the line's `lastShownAt`, the agent note's `dayShownAt` and the stamps of the cards that stand aside, the star ask and the grill-me tip, so none stacks on another and the agent note keeps the gap too; it was one day, and 48 h before that), 3 days after an offer closed with Not now, Escape or a click outside (`inboxApi.revealNotNow`, `notNowAt`; the Board and the Checklist; offers only, so voice's Not now keeps the 40 minutes and the server refuses an update-day id; the agent note does not wait for it; the owner, 2026-11-06: "three after a Not now", which was 4); an offer next for 14 days without its moment retires (`REVEAL_RETIRE_MS`; an update-day dialog never retires, patience lets the others pass it); patience (3 days) as below. An update day's dialogs therefore come 40 minutes or more apart, on any load, in order: sound, the agent note for a new agent, list options (skipped for someone who already picked a look, below), voice, the tab icon, each at its moment. A heavy user's offers then come from day 2 at the same pace, each once its tenure and moment allow: Snooze, Bro mode, the Board (3 days), List view (or earlier, ahead, once the list is taller than the screen) and the Checklist (5 days); several may fall on one day. Patience (3 days) and retirement (14 days) are still counted in days: they are about a moment that does not come, not about the pace.
    - **Code review's offer** (approval "code-review-offer-card", 2026-11-06, `.product/approved/code-review-offer-card/`; the owner picked the Card: "this one needs to show up like immediately", mindful of people who come only to review, no tour). Its slot is one entry, `CODE_REVIEW_OFFER` in `packages/shared/inbox/reveals.ts`: first in the queue, `kind: "offer"`, no tenure and no threads (an offer with no tenure needs no first use either), so on a release build it is `reveal.next` on day 0, ahead of the update-day dialogs; move it by changing that entry. The server lists it in `lineOffers` while config.json `inboxCodeReview` is unset (never decided) and the switch is not held (`switchesHeld`, deleted with `heldSwitch` at 0.29: until then only with PLANNOTATOR_INBOX_PREVIEW=1). The page (`codeReviewOffer.ts` `useCodeReviewOffer`, on `useLineReveal` with `atOnce`: its moment needs no thread read and left and no page age) opens it once the tour's entry screen, its intro and the tour are gone and the page is clear and settled, under `revealHeldBack`; the agent note waits for it (`settled`) and, the gap running, shows on a later load. The dialog (`components/CodeReviewOffer.tsx`, `.ib-cro`): the brand kit's static storm cracked around one sorted column (`codeReviewOfferArt.ts`, three stepped hops, the sorted frame only under reduced motion), "A new way to review", "Filter PRs by your own risk rules", "A review curator walks you through each change" and "See each change as a **Shape**, and where it lands in your **System**" (the owner's words, take 1 of the approval's 15:25Z addendum, with the risk line and the System clause by the owner's call), Not now and "Turn on code review" (stacked on a phone, Turn on on top), "You can change this in Settings." Turn on saves the switch as Settings does (`turnOnFromReveal('code_review')`, the "/" list reloaded); Not now, Escape and a click outside are the line's Not now and leave it off. Every way out is final. Tests: `packages/inbox/codeReviewOffer.test.tsx` (DOM), the offer's cases in `packages/shared/inbox/reveals-schedule.test.ts`.
    - Never while a question waits in the open thread (`openThreadAsks`: the open thread's row has an open question or action item) or a reply is being typed: App's `oneTimeHeldBack` holds the sound, the agent note, list options, voice and every reveal.
    - Each one's moment: the sound, the first page after the update; the agent note, the tour screen gone; Snooze, as below; the Board, back on the list with 5 threads in 2 projects or more (`BOARD_REVEAL_MIN_PROJECTS`); the Checklist, back on the list from a thread where one agent session sent 5 messages or more (`stepsRevealLeaveCounts`, from `noteRevealThread`'s facts of each thread the page loaded); Bro mode, back on the list from a thread whose last agent message has 150 words or more after 20 s or more on it (`broRevealLeaveCounts`, `lastAgentWords` from the same facts); list options, back on the list with no thread open (`listOptionsClear`; this replaces "over a thread" above); voice, a reply of 40 words or more sent from this page in the last minute (`VOICE_INTRO_MIN_WORDS`, api.ts `onReplySent`); voice is never in the line on a computer with no `codex` CLI (looked up once per start, inbox-voice.ts `introAudienceOpen`), so it never holds the others for someone who cannot see it. The notification lines do not wait for list options or voice while their moment is not there.
    - **The tab icon dialog** (the owner approved the mock, 2026-11-09: "Ship it as shown"). An update-day dialog, `tab_icon` in `INBOX_REVEALS` right after voice. Who: decided at the first release start of the version that ships it (`inboxReveals.tab_icon`, the line's own record): someone who used an earlier Inbox and never chose a favicon gets `dueAt`, a fresh install `freshAt` (never asked), and someone who already chose one gets nothing. The server lists it in `lineOffers` while `dueAt` is set and config.json `favicon` is unset, read live, so a pick in Settings (here, plan review or code review) before its moment drops it; the claim keeps the record's `dueAt`, so the same page's retry still shows it. Owner pages only. The page (`tabIconChoice.ts` `useTabIconChoice`, on `useLineReveal` with `atOnce`): 15 s into the page (the owner, 2026-11-10; `soonMayClaim`: the tab visible, the list loaded, no thread to leave and no stillness, under Code review's guards, as the themes dialog above); a pick in Settings on this page drops it (`faviconPickedHere`). The dialog (`components/TabIconChoice.tsx`, the `.ib-cro` shell): the browser's tab strip at real size (this tab's own icon, mirrored from its `<link rel="icon">`, then a plan review and a code review tab), "Which favicon do you prefer?", no body line, Glass, Yellow and Face in Settings' order (64 px from `tabIconArt.ts`, its own script, and the 16 px tab icon), "In use" on the current one, "You can change this in Settings." and one primary button that follows the pick ("Keep Glass", "Use Yellow", "Use Face"). A choice shows on this tab only (`previewFavicon`, nothing saved); the button saves the pick through Settings' own `chooseFavicon` (Keep Glass saves `glass`); Escape and a click outside put back the icon the tab loaded with and save nothing (no Not now: an update-day dialog keeps the 40 minutes). Every way out is final. No shortcut. Stacked on a phone. Tests: `packages/inbox/tabIconChoice.test.tsx` (DOM), the tab icon cases in `packages/server/inbox-reveal.test.ts`, `packages/shared/inbox/reveals-schedule.test.ts`.
    - **Registering a dialog** (how Bro mode, #2693, and List view, #2716, join): (1) add its entry to `INBOX_REVEALS` at its place (Bro after Snooze: `{ id: "bro", kind: "offer", tenureMs: 2 * DAY_MS, minThreads: 0, heavyUse: true, enabled: true }`; List view last, ahead at its moment: `{ id: "list_view", kind: "offer", tenureMs: 2 * DAY_MS, minThreads: 15, ahead: true, enabled: true }`, where `minThreads: 15` is the server's stand-in for "taller than the screen") and its id to `RevealId`, `InboxRevealId` (api.ts) and config.json's `inboxReveals` type (`{ shownAt?, key? }` and `waitingSince`); (2) in packages/server/inbox.ts, add its "undecided" to `lineOffers` (the claim, its `inboxReveals[id]` stamp, retirement and Not now then come from the line; nothing else on the server); (3) on the page, arm it from `lineLatch?.next === id` (an `ahead` entry also from `lineLatch?.ahead.includes(id)`), add its hook's stage to `stagesBusy`, with `useLineReveal` (reveal.ts) and its own `leaveCounts`, `mayClaim` and `holds`, pass `revealBlocked` and `revealHeldBack`, add its stage to `linesFree`, and call `inboxApi.revealNotNow(id)` from its Not now. `heavyUse` reads the dates of the agent messages the person has looked at (`revealHeavyUse`: 10 threads in 7 days, on 2 days or more); no visit is counted.
    - Tests: `packages/shared/inbox/reveals-schedule.test.ts` (30 days of a heavy user, a light user and an update day, with Bro mode and List view in the real queue; List view ahead at its moment; no two within the 40-minute gap, and no waiting for tomorrow or for another load; when to ask again (`revealAgainAt`); the gap after an offer's Not now; retirement, offers only; the agent note once per agent; day 0; heavy use), `packages/server/inbox-reveal.test.ts` ("the schedule's wiring": the load header, Not now offers only, the agent note's agents; an in-process Inbox, so CI runs it), `packages/inbox/tour.test.tsx` (the agent note never over the tour's entry screen, then after it), `packages/inbox/firstRunNotifications.test.tsx` (the sound only when next; the agent note when the line says not on this load, and a refused claim), `packages/inbox/boardReveal.test.tsx` (one project is not the Board's moment).
  - **Reveals for returning users** (approval "inbox-feature-reveal", 2026-11-03, `.product/approved/inbox-feature-reveal/`; the queue in `packages/shared/inbox/reveals.ts`, the page's tracker in `packages/inbox/reveal.ts`). One queue shows people who came back a feature they have not met: Snooze (its announcement, above), then the Board, then the Checklist.
    - **Opt-in** (the owner, 2026-11-04: "Show them the opportunity, give them the opportunity to try it, or just say not now"). A reveal that offers a feature (the Board, the Checklist) never turns it on by showing: the claim only stamps it. Its dialog has two buttons: **Turn it on** (primary) saves the switch as Settings does (`POST /api/inbox/settings` `{ board: { on: true } }` or `{ steps: { on: true } }`, App's `turnOnFromReveal`), so the page follows at once and agents from their next session; **Not now** (ghost), Escape and a click outside close it and write nothing, so the setting stays unset (off) and agent text stays byte-equal to the off state. On a phone the buttons stack with Turn it on on top. A save that fails puts the switch back and shows a toast ("The Board isn’t on" / "The Checklist isn’t on", with Open Settings). People whose Board or Checklist 0.28.49's reveal turned on at the claim (the setting `true`, the reveal stamped) keep it: it is theirs now, and nothing touches it. Snooze's announcement is a plain announcement (Snooze is always there) and is unchanged.
    - The queue (server, read live): `GET /api/inbox/settings` reports `reveal: { next }`, the first entry in order whose "who" holds while the spacing holds. Spacing: never two within 40 minutes on a machine (`REVEAL_SPACING_MS`; one day until 2026-11-09, 48 h until 2026-11-06); every one-time announcement claimed (list options, voice, Snooze, the Board) stamps config.json `inboxReveals.lastShownAt` (a release build only), and the clock is the latest of that and the announcements' own stamps from before it existed (`inboxSnoozeIntro.shownAt`, `inboxBoardIntro.shownAt`, and `inboxListIntro.shownAt` / `inboxVoiceIntro.shownAt` only beside a `dueAt`: without one they are a fresh install's mark, never shown; `revealLastShown`), so an update right after an announcement waits out the day. Patience: an entry that has been next for 3 days (`REVEAL_PATIENCE_MS`, calendar time from `inboxReveals.waitingSince`, the first time it was next) without its moment lets the next qualifying entry go first; it stays in line, and when every qualifying entry has waited that long the one that has waited longest is next. A release build only; no active-days counter. Never for a tailnet guest (`next: null`); the owner's phone counts, and on a tagged machine an allowed login counts as the owner (`isOwnerRequest`).
    - The page arms only `next` (and the `ahead` and `aside` ones beside it), from the load's latch (`reveal.ts` `useLineLatch`, App's `lineLatch`): the newest settings read, each time one arrives. It is held as it is while a one-time dialog of this load is claimed or open (`lineBusy`; the other dialogs are blocked meanwhile, and the line is read again at the claim, so what follows is armed only once the Inbox names it), and a load that had the tour or an update keeps its first read (`snoozeIntroBlockedBy` holds those loads as before). A dialog that closed no longer holds the rest of the load: the Inbox's gap does (a dialog outside the line's hooks, the Board's welcome or the star ask's card, holds the reveals for `REVEAL_TIMES.otherDialogMs`, the same 40 minutes). With no line (`reveal.line` absent: a dev build, an older Inbox) there is no gap, so a load that showed a dialog still shows no other. **No polling** (the owner, 2026-11-09: "We have like a poll or something now ... Could it leak?"): the Inbox reports `reveal.again_in`, the ms until a date can next change the line (`revealAgainAt` in `packages/shared/inbox/reveals.ts`: the end of the gap after each dialog, of an offer's Not now, and for each open entry of its tenure, its patience and an offer's 14 days; the day after first use for the star ask), or null when no date can; the page keeps it on its own clock (App's `lineAgainAt`) and `useLineLatch` holds one `setTimeout` for it, which calls App's `rereadLine` once (a quiet `GET /api/inbox/settings?store=0` that moves only `reveal`, `list_intro`, `snooze_intro` and `voice.intro_due`), whose answer names the next moment or none. Nothing is scheduled when no moment is ahead; the timeout is cleared while the tab is hidden and on `pagehide` (armed again when it is back in front, at once when the moment has passed), while a dialog of the load is open, and on unmount with both listeners. A latch read once at load had left a tab that began while the line was held arming nothing for the rest of its life (the owner, 2026-11-09: list options and List view never shown). Tests: `packages/inbox/lineLatch.test.tsx` (DOM: one read at the named moment and the dialog claimed after it; the line moving past; nothing scheduled with no moment, one timeout for a far one; hidden and back in front; nothing left after `pagehide` and unmount; a tour or update load), `packages/inbox/broRevealApp.test.tsx` (the whole window over a real Inbox: loaded inside the gap, one settings read when it ends, the card opens with no reload), `revealAgainAt`'s cases in `packages/shared/inbox/reveals-schedule.test.ts`, `reveal.again_in` in `packages/server/inbox-reveal.test.ts`.
    - **The idle moment** (the owner, 2026-11-08: someone who keeps a thread open beside the list goes thread to thread and never back to the list, so a moment that waits for "back on the list" never came). `packages/inbox/idleClock.ts` (`useIdleClock`): the page is idle after 20 s (`IDLE_CLOCK.idleMs`) with no pointer move, click, key, wheel, scroll or touch, while the tab is visible and the window focused, with no field focused and nothing open (`PAGE_OVERLAYS`: a dialog, modal or not, a menu, a listbox, an open popover trigger with `aria-haspopup` (an open disclosure such as a Smart group's band only says `aria-expanded` and does not count), the Ask AI window and Show me, an image being marked up, a Shape board, the annotation toolbar and its floats; and App's own hold: a reply typed, a row's menu, a toast, a question waiting in the open thread, a composer, a search, a file or guide). One timeout at a time and passive listeners that only write a time; nothing runs while idle or hidden. App runs one clock, only while a dialog that takes it is armed (`idleClockArmed`, `IDLE_REVEALS`: List view, the Board, the Checklist, Bro mode; and the list options' update-day dialog). Each takes one idle moment per idle spell, besides its leave, once the page is 60 s old (`RevealMomentInput.idle`); input before the claim ends it. The Checklist's idle moment needs the open thread to be a long agent run (`stepsRevealIdleCounts`), Bro mode's a last agent message of 150 words or more (`broRevealIdleCounts`); the list options dialog takes idle as "on the list" (`listOptionsClear`). Snooze, the star ask, Code review, voice, the sound and the agent note keep their own moments: none of them needs the list. Only the moment changed: the line's rules (the spacing, three days after Not now) are the same. The e2e specs that prove the leave moment keep the page busy (`neverIdle`). Tests: `packages/inbox/idleClock.test.tsx`, `listViewReveal.test.tsx`, `boardReveal.test.tsx`, `broReveal.test.tsx`.
    - The Board reveal. Who: first use 3 days ago or more, 5 threads or more (roots in the store; the tour's threads live in the window only and never count), config.json `inboxBoard` still unset (not decided), not shown yet (every undecided owner, the owner's rule "the board we just show every user"). Someone from before the Board was a switch who already saw its first-visit dialog (`inboxBoardIntro.shownAt` set) is offered it the same way; their old stamp is kept. What: **no new dialog** (the owner, 2026-11-03): the claim (`POST /api/inbox/reveal` `{ id: "board", key }`) stamps `inboxReveals.board.shownAt` (with the page's `key`) and the spacing clock in one save and turns nothing on (`inboxBoard` stays unset), then the page opens the Board's existing first-visit dialog as an offer (`BoardIntro` with `onTurnOn`: the animated mini board, its title and first line, without the line about the prompt, Not now and Turn it on in place of Copy prompt). Turn it on writes `inboxBoard: true`, and the sidebar's Board row appears, in every open window (`event: capabilities` carries `board`); a save that fails puts the switch back off and toasts that it is not on (`revealTurnOn.ts`). The claim never stamps the Board's welcome (the owner, 2026-11-04): the first visit to the Board, after Turn it on or after Not now and the switch in Settings later, shows it with Copy prompt; someone who saw it before does not see it again. A tab or device that lost the claim sets its settings' `board.on` only when the answer's `board.on` says another one turned it on. A claim is safe to repeat: the page sends its own `key` (`claimRevealOnce` in `reveal.ts`), the Inbox keeps it with the stamp, and after a network error the page sends the same claim once more (`REVEAL_TIMES.claimRetryMs`), which is shown it again while the switch is still unset; any other key (another tab, the phone) gets `show: false`, and nothing is stamped twice. An answer from the Inbox is final. Once the settings say the Board is on (Settings here, or another window's switch heard live), a pending offer no longer opens (`holds`). When: the Snooze moment's rules (the shared tracker `useRevealMoment`, the times in `REVEAL_TIMES`), with the Board's own need: right after the person leaves a thread they had open 5 s or more and lands back on the list (not on another thread), the list has 5 rows or more (`BOARD_REVEAL_MIN_ROWS`), the page loaded 60 s ago or more, nothing typed or open, no toast, the list settled 2.5 s. Every way out (Turn it on, Not now, Escape, a click outside, a reload) is final; after Not now the Board stays off and Settings → Capabilities is the way on. Once on, the Board's first visit shows its welcome once; its header's "?" still opens it, with Copy prompt.
    - The Checklist reveal (approval "inbox-checklist-reveal", 2026-11-04, `.product/approved/inbox-checklist-reveal/`; `ChecklistIntro` in `packages/inbox/steps/`, `useStepsReveal`): first use 5 days ago or more, 5 threads or more, config.json `inboxSteps` never set, not shown yet; the Board need not be on; the Board's calm moment, spacing and one claim, never a guest. The claim (`claimStepsReveal`) stamps `inboxReveals.steps.shownAt` (with the page's `key`, as the Board's) and the spacing clock in one save and turns nothing on, then the dialog opens as an offer: Not now (ghost) and Turn it on (primary). Turn it on writes `inboxSteps: true`, and agents get the steps text from their next session; after Not now agent text stays byte-equal to a Checklist off. Tests: `packages/shared/inbox/reveals.test.ts`, `packages/server/inbox-reveal.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/inbox-checklist-reveal.spec.ts`.
    - The Bro mode offer (approval "bro-mode", 2026-11-05, `.product/approved/bro-mode/`; its place and moment, approval "inbox-one-time-dialogs": the owner rejected the first build, first in line with no wait, "Something like bro mode is good when there's like ten threads, when you've been in inbox and you've been reading a lot"; `components/BroIntro.tsx`, `useLineReveal` in App): registered with the line as above, right after Snooze, `heavyUse` (10 or more threads read in 7 days, on 2 or more days) and 2 days' tenure, so never on day 0 or 1; the server lists it in `lineOffers` while config.json `inboxBro` is unset. Its moment: back on the list from a thread whose last agent message has 150 words or more, after 20 s or more on it (`broRevealLeaveCounts`). The claim stamps `inboxReveals.bro.shownAt` and the line and turns nothing on. The card is the owner's pick, "poster" (2026-11-05): "Bro mode" set large on sneaker navy, the same in both themes, with the sidebar's Tater sprite sitting on it (played as the sidebar plays it, its first frame under reduced motion); a small "Experimental" chip at the top right (the owner, 2026-11-08; in Settings, Bro mode's row is in Capabilities' Experimental group, under Decision mode, with no chip), the line, "Applies to new agent sessions. In open Claude Code sessions, run `/reload-plugins plannotator`." (the Settings row says the same), then the credit line ("`/bro` as seen by @dillon_mulroy, repo: backnotprop/bro", linking to Dillon's post on x.com and to the skill, `skills/bro/SKILL.md` in `backnotprop/bro`, each in a new tab), then Not now (ghost) and Turn it on (orange). Turn it on writes `inboxBro: true`; Not now, Escape and a click outside send `answer: "not_now"` (the next dialog waits three days) and write no switch, and agent text stays byte-equal to off. Not held by `heldSwitch`: its row shows to everyone. Tests: `packages/server/inbox-bro.test.ts`, `packages/inbox/broReveal.test.tsx`, `packages/inbox/broRevealApp.test.tsx` (the whole window), `packages/shared/inbox/reveals-schedule.test.ts` (Bro in the real queue: a heavy user's day 3, never a light user).
    - "Set the rules" (Rules for your agents, #2194; the owner approved the place and the dialog, 2026-11-11: "Yes, the Settings place and this dialog"; `components/RulesIntro.tsx`, `useLineReveal` in App): registered with the line, last of the later group after Bro mode (`{ id: "agent_rules", kind: "offer", tenureMs: 2 * DAY_MS, minThreads: 10 }`), so no earlier dialog moves; the server lists it in `lineOffers` while no agent has rules (`inboxReminderRulesText` null for every host). Its moment: back from a thread read 5 s or more (the star ask's `starRevealLeaveCounts`) or the page idle, 60 s into the page; rules set meanwhile drop it (`holds`). The card is the Board's and the Checklist's frame (`.ib-bi`) over a picture of an agent's long message that two rules chips ("Keep messages short", "Only when blocked or done") shorten, a stray update fading out and an amber question rising (a 9 s loop of timers that run only while the dialog is open; under reduced motion its last beat, no timers, no transitions), "Set the rules", "Add custom instructions for how you want your agents to use your Inbox.", "They go along with Plannotator's own. Change them any time in Settings.", Not now and "Add your rules" (stacked on a phone). Add your rules closes it and opens Settings → Prompts with the rules editor open (`wantRulesEditor`, agentRules.ts); Not now, Escape and a click outside are the line's Not now. Every way out is final. No shortcut. Settings → Prompts: "Rules for your agents" on top (`components/AgentRules.tsx`: No rules yet and Add rules, or each set box with Edit; the editor is the same frame with one field that starts empty (placeholder "Write your rules, one per line"; no suggested rules: the owner, 2026-11-12), For: All agents / Claude Code / Pi / OpenCode, a count near the room, Back to default), then the reminder folded into one "Reminder text · Default/Custom" row (open when config.json holds text over the cap). Tests: `packages/server/inbox-reveal.test.ts` ("Set the rules"), `packages/shared/inbox/reveals-schedule.test.ts`, `packages/inbox/promptsSettings.test.tsx`, `packages/server/inbox-prompts.test.ts`, `apps/hook/hooks/mod/inbox-reminder-rules.test.ts`.
    - The grill-me tip (`grill_me_tip`; approved by the owner 2026-11-08: "if we have any gap in like consistent use, we should say, hey, have you tried /grill-me yet? ... There's nothing to turn on"; cadence: "Sooner: after 3 answered questions, whatever the days, once"). Beside the line, as the star ask (`aside`), with no tenure, and a `lastResort`: it may be claimed only at a moment the line has nothing else for (no card next and still within its 3 days' patience, none ahead, the star ask settled and not shown that day, the line's own day and spacing free), and its claim stamps only `inboxReveals.grill_me_tip`, so it never moves another dialog's day (pinned by `reveals-schedule.test.ts`: every timeline keeps each dialog's day, the tip on the first free moment after the third answer; a heavy user's on day 7, a light user's on day 18). No Not now for it. Who: someone who answered 3 question cards or more, whatever the days or threads, and shows no sign of grill-me: `revealGrillMeGap` in `packages/shared/inbox/grill-me-tip.ts` (a skip, an unsent pick, an action item or a replaced question is not an answer), and no thread subject, thread name or agent message matching `grill-me` / `grilling`, and no skills block of the person's naming a grill-me skill (`isGrillMeSkill`). No new telemetry: the server reads the store for it only while the tip is unshown, in a release build, and the line has nothing else for the moment, and stops as soon as the answer is known. Its moment, like Bro mode's but keyed to answering: back on the list after 5 s or more on a thread where the person answered a question block (`grillMeTipLeaveCounts`), or the page idle 20 s on such a thread (`IDLE_REVEALS`). `GrillMeTip` (`packages/inbox/components/GrillMeTip.tsx`) is Bro mode's card (`ib-bi ib-bro ib-gm`; no Experimental chip, the owner 2026-11-08) with a small picture of one question card showing what a question holds (two picture choices, the picked one marked Recommended, a `Why <choice>:` line, a note being typed; the owner: the plain three-radio card was "underwhelming"); one button, "Got it". Its title is "Try /grill-me here" (the owner, round 3). Its fine print names the one way in, the Inbox's own skill picker: "Pick /grill-me in a reply or a new message. Nothing to turn on." Got it, Escape and a click outside close it for good with no Not now (the claim stamped it; nothing to turn on). Its words are the `GRILL_ME_TIP_COPY*` constants there (A shown, B kept). It tells people about "The grill-me note" in `inbox-compose.md`. Tests: `packages/shared/inbox/grill-me-tip.test.ts`, `packages/inbox/grillMeTip.test.tsx`, its place in `reveals-schedule.test.ts`.
    - The star ask (approval "star-ask", 2026-11-06, `.product/approved/star-ask/`; `useStarReveal` in `packages/inbox/reveal.ts`, `StarAskCard` in `packages/ui/components/`): aside from the line's order (`aside`: never `next`, never passing or passed by an offer, its claim stamps only `inboxReveals.star`, so no other dialog's day moves; settings `reveal.aside` arms it beside `next`), from the second day of Inbox use (`laterDay`: first use on an earlier local calendar day; the owner, 2026-11-06, "Would rather show it on 2nd day of inbox use"), no thread minimum, only while the line is free (no dialog within the gap, no Not now within three days), the ask never shown or answered on any surface (config.json `starAsk`, shared with plan review, code review and annotate), a release build. Its moment: back on the list after reading a thread 5 s or more, the page open a minute or more, no other reveal showing or claiming. Once its card is up, the other reveals hold back for the gap (`noteOneTimeDialogShown`, read by App's `revealBlocked`, `REVEAL_TIMES.otherDialogMs`; the Inbox keeps the same 40 minutes from `inboxReveals.star.shownAt`), not for the rest of the load. The claim runs the already-starred check with the person's gh before it stamps anything. Not a dialog: a corner card bottom-right (a named region, no focus trap, no key closes it), the Type Only art band (a `#F3D45E` star behind "star it?"). Star on GitHub stars with gh on the click (`POST /api/inbox/star-ask`), then the star fills `#FFC926`, the word becomes "starred." and the card leaves 4 s later; without gh (missing, signed out, an error) the button is a plain link to the repository. Not now records the answer and stamps nothing of the line. Tests: `packages/shared/star-ask.test.ts`, `packages/server/inbox-star-ask.test.ts`, `packages/shared/inbox/reveals*.test.ts`, `packages/inbox/starReveal.test.ts`, `packages/ui/components/StarAskCard.test.tsx`.
    - Tests: `packages/shared/inbox/reveals.test.ts` (the order, the one-day boundary, the clock as the latest stamp, patience passing a stuck Snooze and Snooze next again after, both stuck: the longest wait goes, tenure and thread boundaries, dev builds never), `packages/server/inbox-reveal.test.ts` (day 4: `card` not listed and agent text byte-equal to a Board switched off before the claim and after it with Not now, listed after Turn it on; the one save, with nothing turned on; never over `true` or `false`, nor over a Board 0.28.49's reveal turned on; two claims at once; 405, 409, a bad id; dev build; tenure and threads; a guest never, the owner's phone and a tagged machine's allowed login do, for the reveal and the Board's first-visit dialog; Snooze first, its spacing and its stamp; patience; the list options claim counting; an upgrade with Snooze and voice shown an hour ago and no `inboxReveals`: nothing due; fresh-install marks not counting; a `BoardIntro` already seen: offered too, nothing on, the old stamp kept, no welcome once turned on, and `false` with that stamp never claimed; the claim never stamping the welcome, so the first visit after Turn it on, or after Not now and Settings, shows it once; a lost answer: the same key shown it again, no other key, tab or device, one stamp and the spacing kept; the Checklist's claim turning nothing on, Not now byte-equal to off and Turn it on the steps text; a dev build's claim not stamping the clock), `packages/inbox/boardReveal.test.tsx` (DOM: the moment back on the list and not on another thread, the offer turning nothing on and Turn it on saving the switch and drawing the Board row, each blocker, a refused claim, a claim lost to another tab that turned the Board on still drawing the row, App's load latch `revealArmed` and its older-Inbox fallback, every other way out final with the Board still off and nothing saved, a reload; a lost claim answer sent again once with the same key, a 500 never; the offer not opening once another window turned the Board on, nor claiming when the settings say it is on; a failed save taking the row away with the toast; `ChecklistIntro`'s two buttons, Escape and a click outside), `packages/inbox/revealTurnOn.test.ts` (a failing save: the switch back off before the settings re-read, the approved toast and Open Settings), `packages/inbox/capabilities.test.ts` (a Board frame through the page's frame reader onto the held settings), `tests/e2e/inbox-board-reveal.spec.ts` (a compiled release binary seeded at day 4 by `inbox-board-reveal-seed.ts`: never on load, once at the moment as an offer with nothing on and no Board row, a second tab and a reload never, Not now leaving it unset; a 390 px phone with the buttons stacked, Turn it on writing it on, the row in the sheet and the first visit with its welcome; shots in light and dark in `PLANNOTATOR_E2E_PROOF_DIR/board-reveal`).

  **Open counts** (approval "inbox-row-action-icon", 2026-10-29, A2 and Q3; fill steps, approval "inbox-question-mark", 2026-11-08): what waits on the person, when the row has no words. Open action items are the action box (`ActionBadge` in `components/RowBadge.tsx`, `[data-action-count]`): the action checklist card's `todo` box with its tick taken out (`todoOpen` in `icons.tsx`) and the number, in the badge's pill shape with a neutral fill (`--ib-badge-act-bg`, the text colour at 8% on light, 10% on dark; outlined with no fill when Soft badges are off) and the text colour. Open questions (action items left out) are the question mark, `?N` (`QuestionMark`, `.ib-qn`): Lucide's message-circle-question-mark speech bubble (ISC) right next to its number, quiet text, no pill. Each takes one of three fill steps (`rowMarks`, `data-wait-step`; see "Row badges" below). The box sits before `?N`, both before the time (a wide row's `.ib-cq`) or at line 3's end (three-line and phone rows). Each keeps its words as visually hidden text, the row's accessible name and the tooltip (not on a phone): "2 actions to do", "1 question". Past 99 each draws "99+" (`shownCount`), its words keep the exact count ("128 actions to do", "Holding up work: 104 questions"); a pair too wide for its 84 px column and the gap ("[□ 99+] ?99+") runs to the left, under the badge column, whose words (when drawn) paint above it on an opaque ground (`--ib-row-fill`). No open action item: no box. A row with words (a draft, Undelivered, Read …) draws its words instead, as before, and keeps only a filled mark, so a blocked row always shows its state; so does Settings → Rows' Status mark, the filled mark before it. Tests: the row case in `packages/inbox/actionChecklist.test.tsx` (DOM), `packages/inbox/rowBadges.test.tsx` (DOM), `tests/e2e/inbox-actions.spec.ts`, `tests/e2e/inbox-row-badges.spec.ts`.

  **Row badges** (approvals "inbox-row-badges", 2026-10-25, and "inbox-question-mark", 2026-11-08, Take 3 "Fill steps", with today's theme colours; `components/RowBadge.tsx`): there is no separate Stopped / Holds up badge (the old ⚠ triangle and its held-up count). The row's state is the fill step of its marks: open, the quiet unfilled `?N` (and the neutral action box); holding up work, the same face in today's Holds up tint pill (`.ib-badge.ib-b-hold`: `--ib-badge-hold-bg`, `--ib-badge-hold-fg`); stopped on you, today's Stopped tint pill (`.ib-b-stop`). The number stays the open questions (or open action items on the box). Each mark's step comes from its own `waits` (a question's or action item's `Stopped:` / `Holds up:`), so an action item that holds up work fills the box while the question mark stays open, and a thread stopped only by an action item shows it on the box with no question mark; when the waits do not carry the row's section (an older Inbox sends none; they are capped; a Done or Can't do it still in the reply draft keeps the agent stopped) the state goes on the question mark, else on the box (with nothing open, the staged count). Words, the mark's visually hidden name and tooltip: "3 questions", "Holding up work: 5 questions", "Stopped on you: 4 questions", "Stopped on you: 1 action item" (the section labels, so the band and the mark agree). A list of 1150 px or more also draws "Stopped" / "Holds up 3" in the badge column (`WaitWords`, `[data-wait-words]`, `aria-hidden`: the mark and the row's description say it) while `WIDE_ROW_WAIT_WORDS` is true (today; whether they stay is open with the owner); false leaves them to the card, the tooltip and the name. Narrower wide rows, three-line and phone rows draw the marks alone. The held-up count lives in the card. Accessibility floor (shots, 2026-11-08): no fill against a fill reads in greyscale and under deuteranopia and protanopia, but the two tints are close in lightness, so Stopped and Holds up differ by hue alone (the section band and the words carry it). Hover card A (Halftone's Card `divided` on a Popover surface, Inbox tokens; `WaitCard`, `.ib-wcard` in `inbox.css`): a header (the mark's glyph in the badge's tint, the bubble, or the box when only an action item blocks (`data-wait-card-glyph`); "Stopped" / "Holds up N", the thread's name), one row per stopped reason or held-up item with an inset hairline ("Also holds up N" under a Stopped card's reasons), Q1/A1 labels only when the items come from more than one question (the question's place in its message; numbered in order when two would read alike), at most five lines then "+N more", and a quiet footer with the agent's mark, name and time. It is ui's Tooltip through `Tip`: it opens on a filled mark (and a wide row's words) after the usual delay, and while the row has keyboard focus (on the words when drawn, else the strongest filled mark) (`:focus-visible` on the row; ui Tooltip's additive `forceOpen`, Tip's `open`); a click's focus opens nothing. The row names the card's words through `aria-describedby` (a `hidden` span beside the row, `badgeDescription`). Rows beside a thread (three-line) keep the card, on the marks at line 3's right end; a phone row draws the marks only, no card, in the same place. The card reads the row's additive `waits` (see "Inbox Server"); an older Inbox's row has none and the card lists the merged `questions.holds_up`. The card sizes to its content (owner, 2026-10-31): as wide as its longest line (a row's label, the header with the thread's name), at least 296 px, at most 460 px (a 60-character label at 13 px is about 400 px, plus the Q label and padding), and never past the viewport less its 16 px gutter; only a line past the cap wraps (`text-wrap: pretty`). The thread-name chip takes the room (capped at 260 px, ends in an ellipsis, full name in its title). Tests: `tests/e2e/inbox-badge-card-width.spec.ts` (`PLANNOTATOR_BADGE_CARD_SHOTS`), `packages/inbox/rowBadges.test.tsx` (DOM), `packages/server/inbox-row-waits.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/inbox-row-badges.spec.ts` (screenshots in `PLANNOTATOR_ROW_BADGES_SHOTS`).

  **Delivery ticks** (approval "inbox-row-delivery", "Ticks", 2026-10-20; `components/DeliveryTick.tsx`, words in `packages/inbox/rowDelivery.ts`): a row whose latest sent item is the person's (their reply or message is the last message, the Inbox's notes read past, or a 👍 Looks good on the latest agent message once it is not withdrawn) shows a glyph before its time, on wide rows, rows beside a thread and phone rows (and, while it waits on a busy agent, "Undelivered N min" in place of the count, see "Undelivered replies go in by themselves"): a clock queued, three dots sending (they brighten in turn; still under `prefers-reduced-motion`), ✓ saved (an agent that reads the thread later, or the session that asked ended), ✓✓ delivered (the row's time is then the delivery time), a warning triangle not delivered. Shape tells the state, the tone only helps (`--ib-tick-*` in `inbox.css`, stepped toward the text colour like the Soft badges' words, light and dark). The state in words is in the row's accessible name (the glyph is `role="img"` with `aria-label` "Queued for Claude Code,", "Looks good, delivered to Pi,", …), and its tooltip (ui's, `Tip`) is the thread's own delivery line word for word (`deliveryText`). The row reads the server's `delivery` field (see "Inbox Server"), so a row and its thread never disagree; Send now stays in the thread. A Sent row now shows its own subject; a reply that tags agents keeps its recipients words; an older Inbox (no `delivery`) keeps the old words, "Queued for <agent>; it gets this as its next turn." / "Saved for <agent>. …" (`sentRowLine`). The glyph sits in the 10 px gap left of the 76 px time column (`overflow: visible`), so the columns do not move. Live: a turn starting or ending writes no store line, so the server compares the live sessions' delivery signature every second while a window listens and sends `event: deliveries` (windows only, never the phone's door); the window reads the list again then, only while some row's delivery can still move (not delivered, and not saved for an agent that only reads the thread, which only a store line changes). Quiet is folded. The Soft look paints the list without moving it (owner, 2026-10-16: Soft modernizes today's Inbox; today's densities, row heights, columns and places win over the prototype's): section bands keep their 34 px band (on `--ib-soft-band`: the raised surface at radius 8 when on, today's muted band when off, the owner's choice), the thread chip is a quiet pill in the window's face (`--ib-soft-chip`; off, today's outlined monospace tag) and the badges tinted pills (`.ib-b-stop`, `.ib-b-hold`), each the same box as before (a border that goes stays as transparent width).
- Rendering cost with thousands of rows: every row stays in the DOM (no virtualization: find in page, focus, the accessibility tree and the scrollbar behave as for any page). A row off screen skips style, layout and paint (`content-visibility: auto` on `.ib-lbody > .ib-group > .ib-rw` and `> .ib-mrow`, with `contain-intrinsic-size: auto …` estimates of 50 px wide, 84 px three-line, 66 px + padding on a phone; `auto` keeps the size a row last rendered at), and rows are memoized on their content (`ListRow` in `InboxList.tsx`: a list read brings new row objects, so it compares them deeply, with callbacks of one identity and today's date, which `shortTime` writes against), so a list update renders only the rows that changed. Measured on 4,000 threads in headless Chromium: an agent message arriving went from about 370 ms of long tasks to none, Show from about 600 ms to 70 ms, and style and layout at load from about 230 ms to 70 ms.
- The look of a row (design record "inbox-polish", approved 2026-10-15, not in this repo; `inbox.css`, `components/InboxList.tsx`):
  - **Bold means news you have not seen** (the owner, 2026-11-08: "if they could unbolden after I at least read the latest message ... Then they just stay where they are"; the row's `unread`, `inboxRowUnread` in `packages/shared/inbox/list.ts`). A row in Stopped, Holding up work or Waiting on you is bold until the person has seen its latest agent message (`unseen` 0: they opened the thread), then reads as read in its group, with its badge and open counts; an agent's next message makes it bold again. Opening it unbolds it at once (held.ts `lookedRow`, before the server's look lands). Its hover action stays Mark read (set the questions aside, to Quiet), never Mark unread. A row in New is bold except one there only for the person's own draft. Projects' `unread` counts follow `unread`, so a read thread that still waits is not counted there, as on the tab (`tabCount.ts`); the sidebar's counts are the waiting groups and do not change. Proofs: `packages/inbox/readWaitingRow.test.tsx` (a real store drawn by the real list), `held.test.ts` (`markHeldRowLooked`), and the thread-state model in `packages/shared/inbox/store.test.ts` (invariant 7, the trace fixtures).
  - Hover and the selected row are a pill 3 px inside the row (its `::before`, radius 8, `--ib-lr-sm` with `--ib-soft-shape`; no border; variant A): hover muted 45% (`--ib-hover`), selected `--ib-sel` (primary 10% light, 15% dark; 14% / 19% hovered). The selection is the owner's pick of today's (`--ib-soft-selection` off; on, a neutral fill, the text colour at 7%, 10% hovered). Rows have no line between them in the Soft look (`--ib-soft-rows` on in both modes, owner 2026-10-17, after the prototype: the colour study had kept today's hairlines): each row keeps its separator's 1 px as a transparent border, so the pitch is today's and rows part by the pill's 3 px inset and the space around it; the wide, three-line and phone (`.ib-mrow`) rows alike. Off, the separators are today's hairlines and run unbroken under the pill.
  - A named thread's row draws its name as the title and its subject as the preview, even when the subject starts with the name (approval "list-rows-mail-icons": the approved "Okta SSO" row); a subject that only repeats the name is not drawn twice. Rows no longer draw the thread chip, so the rule that left it out (`chipName`) went with it; the thread's meta line keeps it (`threadChipName`).
  - A wide row's title, preview or project label, or a three-line row's preview, cut with "…" shows the whole text in ui's `Tooltip` on hover (`ClipText` over `Tip`, only while cut; 150 ms, the next one at once within 400 ms). No native `title` on them, not a focus stop, nothing added for a screen reader (the row's name holds the whole text). Beside a thread the project is cut the same way, with its tooltip (approval "inbox-list-row-v2"); on a phone it is cut with no tooltip. Beside a thread and on a phone the title wraps instead and is never cut. The preview's flex-shrink is 1,000,000 (was 1,000): flex shares a shortfall by factor times width, so at 1,000 the item before it (then the thread chip) still lost a fraction of a pixel whenever it shrank, and Chrome drew an ellipsis on text that fit (the owner's "reconciliation-r…", a 0.06 px cut at a device scale of 2 beside an open thread); the list header's folder and compose's To picker folder take the same factor.
  - **Tooltips are components, never native `title`s** (the owner, 2026-10-17: "I want an actual component tooltip"). Every hover text in the Inbox window is `Tip` (`packages/inbox/components/Tip.tsx`) over ui's `Tooltip`; the window's one `TooltipProvider` (`InboxApp`) sets 150 ms (`TIP_DELAY_MS` = ui's `TOOLTIP_DELAY_MS`, ui's default for every app; also passed per trigger) and 400 ms to the next. `cut` opens it only while the text is cut, `when` decides otherwise (a mark whose label is hidden), else it always opens; where the native title said something the screen does not (a sidebar project's folder: `ProjectTipText`, name over folder; a named search chip's subject; a full date) it always opens. `isTextCut` counts any whole pixel of scrollWidth over clientWidth, and compares the text's Range width with the box's content width (a hundredth of a pixel), so a sub-pixel ellipsis still opens it. A control that can be disabled (`disabled` in its props) hangs the tooltip on a `.ib-tipwrap` span (`flex: none`, so it never shrinks below the control it holds) whose disabled child takes no pointer, since a disabled button gets no pointer events. A trigger inside an open native `<dialog>` (search, compose, the agent-note and update dialogs) portals the popup into that dialog (ui `Tooltip`'s additive `container` prop), else into the window's root (`.pn-inbox`, marked `data-pn-tooltip-root`; ui's `tooltipContainerOf`, which ui's `HostTitle` uses too), so the window's tokens reach it: with Soft's `--ib-soft-float` on, a tooltip (`[data-pn-tooltip]`, ui's popup) floats like the window's menus and the toast (Soft's shadow); off, ui's own float shadow (`--float-shadow`). ui's popup has no visible border either way (the owner's design-system ruling q4: a borderless float at 150 ms in every product; the 1 px border is transparent so forced colors still draw an edge), and the sidebar edge's own tooltip (`.ib-edge-tip`) uses `--float-shadow` too. Nothing opens under `(hover: none)`. Escape closes an open tooltip and still reaches the window (ui `Tooltip`'s additive `escapePassesThrough`), so Escape closes a thread even while a tooltip shows (Base UI's default swallows it). Triggers keep their own `aria-label`; a sidebar project keeps its folder as its accessible description (`aria-describedby` on a `hidden` element). ui's Tooltip draws at `z-[110]`, above the annotation toolbar (`z-[100]`). ui's own hover text inside the Inbox goes the same way through `configurePlannotatorUI({ componentTooltips: true })` (ui `components/HostTitle`; default false, so plan review, annotate, code review and other hosts keep native titles): the annotation panel's and toolbar's buttons and labels (a title that was a button's only name becomes its `aria-label`; any other title stays the control's description, a `hidden` element named by `aria-describedby`, since the tooltip tags nothing for a screen reader), and a message link to an attachment (`resolveLocalLink`'s `title`). Left native: an iframe's `title` (its accessible name), and the ui components not yet routed through `HostTitle` that the Inbox can show (CommentPopover, AnnotationToolstrip, the ImageAnnotator toolbar, CodeBlock's copy button, TableToolbar / TablePopout, DiagramBlock and the diagram viewer, CodeFilePopout, QuestionsPanelSection, HtmlViewer's own controls). `clipTitle.ts` is gone. Proofs: `packages/inbox/componentTooltips.test.tsx` (DOM: a cut row label, a sidebar project and its description, the 0.06 px chip), `packages/inbox/links.test.tsx` (an attachment link's component tooltip), `tests/e2e/inbox-tooltips.spec.ts` (compiled binary at 1440, device scale 2, a thread open: no native title anywhere in the window but iframes, the tooltips, no chip cut by under a pixel at six list widths, Escape with a tooltip open, Mark read never spilling over Archive from 320 to 1440 px, a disabled control's tooltip).
  - **A list row's tooltips mount on first use** (`RowTip`, `packages/inbox/components/RowTip.tsx`; the 2026-11-02 XL trace: about 2,500 row tooltips mounting cost about 100 ms of opening a thread from the list, and as much on every row redraw). Inside the rows (`LazyRowTips`, which `InboxList` sets around its sections) a `RowTip` draws its trigger alone, with base-ui's `data-base-ui-tooltip-trigger`, until a mouse or pen enters it or it takes keyboard focus, then mounts the same `Tip`. A pointer's entry arms it in the next task, never inside the event: a press with no hover before it (a pen's first contact) brings its own over events, and a trigger drawn again there took the press's down with the old element, so no click reached the row. A press that reaches the unarmed trigger arms nothing, and nothing arms it until the pointer leaves (not the press's focus, nor an over event while the button is down or a compatibility mouseover sent after the down), as `Tip` let a press cancel its open; the next entry arms it. The trigger element is drawn again when it arms, so the pointer's `mouseenter`, and its last move there if one came, are sent to the new one (`flushSync` has already run the effects that add base-ui's listeners): the 150 ms rest and the 400 ms instant run stay base-ui's own. It arms on the over events too, since React derives no enter when the element the pointer left was itself just drawn again, and rows drawn again under a pointer at rest get the browser's `mouseover` with no move: as with `Tip`, nothing opens until the pointer moves. Because the browser may never send the new element a `mouseleave` (a row's hover action showing over the tick the pointer just reached), the first real mouse event over anything else sends base-ui its `mouseleave` (`watchLeave`). A touch, its tap's mouse events included, and any pointer on a screen with no hover never arm a trigger. A row's hover action (Mark read, Mark unread, Send now) is armed by its row (`RowWithAction`, pointer or focus into `.ib-rw`), so a Tab never moves focus onto a trigger that is about to be drawn again; a trigger that arms on its own focus gets the focus back. A tip given `open` (the badge card, shown while its row has keyboard focus) and every tip outside the rows stay `Tip`. A DOM test that sizes a trigger arms it first (`armTip` in `packages/inbox/testing/tooltip.ts`). Proof: `packages/inbox/rowTip.test.tsx` (no hover listener in drawn rows, the first entry opens the tooltip from a trigger drawn the same, Tab onto the action keeps focus, a touch redraws nothing).
  - Keyboard focus (`:focus-visible` only) is one 2 px `--ring`: row-like items draw it inside themselves following their radius (list rows on the pill; the Quiet band, sidebar rows and projects, a phone's row, fold lines and a message's head with `outline-offset: -2px`), so no neighbour paints over it and the projects' scroll box never clips it. List rows keep a transparent 2 px outline beside the inset ring, so forced colours (Windows High Contrast) still draw one. Free-standing controls keep the base ring, 2 px outside; `button.ib-mut` (Reply, the hold-ups toggle) has radius 6. Measured at 4.9:1 or more against the card, the hover and the selected pill, light and dark.
- Beside an open thread the list narrows to 380 px and takes the sidebar's edge (`shell/ListColumnEdge.tsx`: the same `useResizablePanel`, `CollapsibleEdge` separator and `ResizeHandle`, without the collapse; arrows, Home/End, `aria-controls` the column; its grab area leans toward the pane so a classic scrollbar stays grabbable; cookie `plannotator-inbox-list-width`): a drag widens it from 380 px up to leaving the thread pane 480 px (no edge when the window leaves no room to widen, and none below 768 px), and its rows turn wide while the column's border box is 900 px or wider (`WIDE_ROWS_MIN_LIST_WIDTH`, read from the observer's `borderBoxSize`, the same box as `getBoundingClientRect`) (`tests/e2e/inbox-list-resize.spec.ts`).
- **The thread panel view** (the owner's ask, 2026-11-01, after Google Chat's split pane; `packages/inbox/threadPanel.ts`): an optional layout where the list keeps the short width it has beside a thread with no thread open, and the thread's place on the right shows `components/EmptyThreadPanel.tsx`. The wide view stays the default and is unchanged: with the choice off the window draws nothing new.
  - The choice: "Keep thread panel open", the one checkbox of the list's ⋮ menu group "Thread panel" (between "Rows" and "Machines"; `TidyMenu`'s `threadPanel`, `data-thread-panel-option`, `closeOnClick={false}`, a plain checkbox as its neighbours are). It takes effect at once, so it is also the way to open the panel with no thread open; there is no second control, because a control in the list's header would move the wide view. Remembered per browser in the cookie `plannotator-inbox-thread-panel` through `storage` ("1" while on, removed when off), never on the server. It is not a look option (`listLook.ts`): the window's layout reads it, the one-time list options dialog does not offer it, and its Done never writes it. No shortcut.
  - The rule (`threadPanelShows`): the choice, on a window that is not a phone and does not stack (`stack.thread`). `App.tsx` turns it into `InboxList`'s `narrow`, the same prop an open thread sets, so the column, its resize edge and the rows are the ones beside a thread. The panel is a plain `.ib-pane` (`.ib-pempty`, `data-thread-panel-empty`), the pane a thread fills, so opening or closing a thread moves nothing in the list. Every close (×, Escape, the Inbox row, a missing thread's Close) leaves `route.thread` null, which is the empty panel. Focus and history go through today's code, which reads `stack.thread` and `view`, not this choice.
  - A reload: the choice is read before the first render, and while the list loads the window draws the two panes blank (`data-thread-panel-loading`; `ListColumnEdge` sets a dragged width), so no frame shows the wide list, and no frame shows the empty panel under a `#thread=` link or before a first-run screen.
  - Where it does not show: a phone (no panel, and no item: `threadPanel` is not passed), a stacked window (the wide list and today's stacking; the item stays, as Compact rows stays on a wide row, and the choice applies once there is room), the tour (it draws its own `InboxList`, without the item), the first-run screen of an empty Inbox, Settings, the Board, a card's thread, What's new, Drafts. A file beside the thread takes the list's place as it always did.
  - The empty panel: one quiet line, "No thread selected", centered in the pane in the muted text color (`.ib-pempty`), the approved quiet empty state. Nothing in it takes focus.
  - Sharing: `threadPanel.ts` holds no routes or server calls and `EmptyThreadPanel` takes no props, so Workspaces can share the rule and the empty panel when it adds this. Nothing in `packages/ui` changes.
  - Proof: `packages/inbox/threadPanel.test.tsx` (DOM: the cookie, the rule, the item) and `tests/e2e/inbox-thread-panel.spec.ts` (the compiled binary: the cases above except a card's thread, What's new and Drafts, a frame-by-frame reload, J and K, search, a linked machine).
- **The List view** (approval "inbox-list-view", 2026-11-05, `.product/approved/inbox-list-view/`, not in this repo; issue #2714; `packages/inbox/listView.ts`): one list, newest first, with today's rows and badges (Stopped, Holds up) and no section bands. Smart groups (today's grouped list) stays the default.
  - The choice: the list's ⋮ menu group "View" with "Smart groups" and "Simple list" (the owner's words, 2026-11-05), right after Refresh (`TidyMenu`'s `view`, `data-list-view`): a `Menu.RadioGroup` labelled by its group label, two `menuitemradio` items with `aria-checked`, drawn as the menu's checkboxes are with a round box and a dot (`.ib-pmi-box[data-radio]`). A pick changes the list at once and leaves the menu open. Remembered per browser in the cookie `plannotator-inbox-list-view` through `storage` ("list" while Simple list is on, removed for Smart groups), as the thread panel is; never on the server, never in the look options. The tour (`rememberLook` false) draws the groups and offers no choice. No shortcut, no setting.
  - What List draws (`flatRows`): every row of every group but the Snoozed band in one `.ib-group` (`data-section-id="list"`, `data-flat-list`), so nothing the groups show is hidden (Quiet's rows are drawn, not folded; Sent's too); the snoozed keep the groups' rule, their folded Snoozed band under the rows; archived threads stay out. Each row keeps the group the held list draws it in (`drawn`) for its actions. ↑ ↓ Home End, J/K and the row menu read the DOM order, so they follow List.
  - Held order applies (held.ts): agents' messages never reorder List. A row keeps its place while it keeps its group, whatever its time does in place (the person's Send in the open thread, Mark read's note); only a sort of the whole list moves rows: `App.tsx` moves `listSort` (`ListSort`, `sortedWhole`) on every settle and adopt (Show, a close, a refresh, another folder), never for a row shown in place or one row moved alone (a snooze, a phone's Back), which takes its place by its time. A refresh beside an open thread keeps that thread's place (`keep`).
  - "N new · Show" stays where it is in both views, above the rows (`HeldNoticeLine`; the owner kept its place and look, 2026-11-05). List works out its rows only while it shows (`flatRows` behind `asList`): a switch to List is a sort of the whole held list. App keeps the sections it hands the list between renders that change none of them (`listSections`).
  - The choice dialog (`components/ListViewIntro.tsx`; the owner, 2026-11-05: "smart groups versus simple list … we recommend the smart groups, but you could also treat it like a simple email list"; approval "inbox-list-view-dialog") is registered with the schedule of one-time dialogs ("The schedule of one-time dialogs" above): `list_view`, last in `INBOX_REVEALS` and `ahead` (`minThreads: 15`, 2 days of use), armed from `reveal.next` or `reveal.ahead` with `useLineReveal` (approval "inbox-one-time-dialogs", the change: once the list is taller than the screen, on Grouped, at the next calm moment). Its moment is back on the list from a thread (`listViewRevealLeaveCounts`), or the page idle in any layout ("The idle moment" above); its need is the list loaded, not the first-run screen, 60 s into the page, on Smart groups and the list's body taller than it shows (`listViewRevealMayClaim`, `listTallerThanScreen`); `holds` drops it once Simple list is picked. The line's claim stamps it once per Inbox (`inboxReveals.list_view`); the server never sees the view (each browser's own), so `lineOffers.list_view` is always undecided. It opens with no thread open, or over an open thread while the page is idle (`listViewRevealClear`); never over a question waiting in the open thread. A fair choice between two equals, in two columns: "Which view do you prefer?", "You can change this any time in the ⋮ menu.", then each column a small picture of the person's own rows (the list options dialog's `PreviewList`, light for everyone, scaled down: the groups with their bands, and the same threads newest first), its name, one line and a button. Smart groups is tagged "Recommended" and its button ("Use Smart groups") is the primary one; "Use Simple list" is plain. The view the list draws now is tagged "In use" (it follows the view). Focus starts on the question. "Use Simple list" switches at once through the ⋮ menu's own setter (`useListView`); "Use Smart groups" keeps the groups; Escape and a click outside keep the current view and are the line's Not now (`inboxApi.revealNotNow('list_view')`: the next dialog waits three days). No shortcut, no setting.
  - Proof: `packages/inbox/listView.test.ts` (the rows, held order, the sort, the cookie), `packages/inbox/listViewMenu.test.tsx` (DOM: the menu's radios and their state, every thread in List, the remembered choice, the tour, "N new · Show" above the rows in both views, a row archived in place faded and out of its count, the empty state after Archive all), `packages/inbox/listViewReveal.test.tsx` (DOM: the choice's moment and every blocker, both pictures, the primary button, "In use" following the view, Use Simple list, Use Smart groups, Escape, once per browser), `tests/e2e/inbox-list-view.spec.ts` (the compiled binary: news reorders no row in both views and on a 390 px phone, every thread the groups show, a reload, ↓, the hover Mark read with news waiting, Undo, Show, a snooze made elsewhere moving only its row, an Archive's Undo after the tidy; PNGs in `PLANNOTATOR_LIST_VIEW_SHOTS`, a recording with `PLANNOTATOR_LIST_VIEW_VIDEO`) and `tests/e2e/inbox-list-view-intro.spec.ts` (a release build named by `PLANNOTATOR_E2E_RELEASE_BINARY`, skipped without one: the choice at 1440 and 390, Use Simple list, Use Smart groups, once).
- **The list's filter** (the owner, 2026-11-08: "it needs to be the classic filter icon and nothing else in the header under any state. A number badge can show and the filter icon can change color"; `packages/inbox/listFilter.ts` holds the rule, `components/ListFilter.tsx` draws it).
  - The header: the filter icon alone (three lines narrowing, Lucide's `list-filter`; the owner's pick on #2921), left of Search, New message and ⋮, at every width (`data-list-filter`). With picks it takes the accent colour and a small count in its corner, one per ticked row (`data-list-filter-count`, up to "9+"; its name "Filter, N active"). The count is `components/CountBadge.tsx` (`.ib-cnt` in a `.ib-cnt-host`): the PRs button's count from PR linking, 13 px across, 9.5 px bold, `--muted-foreground` fill and `--background` digits, centred both ways (tabular figures, a line as tall as the badge); PR linking's `.ib-prc-n` moves onto it when it lands. No tokens, chips or line over the list, ever. The tour draws no funnel.
  - The menu (Base UI's keys; the Pull requests filter's look, `.ib-pc-menu`; a phone's bottom sheet titled "Filter"): Not seen (below) first, then Updated (Today, Last 7 days, Last 30 days, Custom range… with From, To, Apply), Agent (several; only with two agents or more), Pull request (below), then Project last, since the sidebar already narrows by project (the owner, 2026-11-08; each project, several; only on the Inbox page with two projects or more), then "Clear filters". Each row shows how many threads it would leave with the other groups' picks, and a tick. Picks inside a group widen; groups narrow each other. The menu stays open while picking. "Updated" is the thread's `last_at` in calendar days of the window's clock: Today from midnight, Last 7 days is today and the six before it.
  - Not seen (the owner's name and shape on #3226: one row, on or off, no heading; the row menu's envelope): only the rows drawn bold, the row's `unread` (`ib-u`; shared/inbox/list.ts `inboxRowUnread`: Stopped, Holding up work and Waiting on you while an agent message is unseen, New unless only the person's unsent words keep it there; never Sent or Quiet), read from the rows as drawn (a look on its way and a drawn Mark read included). Not "Unread": Mark read sends a thread to Quiet, and a row still waiting on the person is seen once its news is. Always offered; one in the badge when on; the cookie's `notSeen: true`. A row that stops matching by the person's own Mark read or Mark unread leaves at once, as the action moves it; one that changes elsewhere (seen on a phone) leaves at the next list read, as a Pull request pick's row does. A row opened while bold stays where it is until the list settles (App.tsx `heldRead`, `filterListSections`' `heldRead`; settle.ts: closing the thread, Show, another folder or page, the next thread on a phone), so opening a row never takes it out of a Not seen list.
  - Pull request (Open, Draft, Merged, Closed, Has a PR, No PR; several; on since PR linking shipped). The group is offered once a thread in the list has a PR, and while a pick of it is remembered (so it can be unticked), as Agent and Project wait for two. It reads a row through `rowPullStates`: the row's `pulls` (`InboxThreadPullsSummary`: `count` and the `headline` PR's `state`, the stored state the server's reader last wrote; the list request reads the PRs still in play again off the request path, and a changed state arrives as a `thread_pull` line that reloads the list). So a thread is "Open" when the PR its row's mark shows is open (the first still open or a draft, else one not read yet, else the last); no field or `count` 0 is No PR. A PR not read yet (`unknown`: before the reader's first answer, or with no gh or glab) is kept by "Has a PR" alone, never by a state or by No PR.
  - Where it applies: App.tsx filters what `filterList` gives the list (`filterShown`), so Smart groups count only the matches and a group left empty is not drawn, Simple list shows the same matches, J/K and ↓ ↑ read the rows on screen, and "N new" counts only news in threads the filter shows. The sidebar's counts never follow it. The sidebar's project stays a page of its own: there the Project group is not offered and the Project picks step aside (they neither filter nor count, and Clear filters keeps them for Inbox).
